## PR — Repo-local `.flow/` scratch + handoff stamping (v1.27.0, **MERGED as #88**, `783c9fcc`, FB-0082)

**Restated request:** Part B of the consumer dogfood report: make flow's cross-process ephemeral state visible to the process that has to read it, and impossible to confuse with another workspace's. Placed at the TOP of this doc deliberately — `extract-criteria.py` / `extract-visual-states.py` read only the FIRST block of their kind, so an active PR's Spec-walk must precede every retained one (`plan-discipline.md` § Active-block placement). `/flow:audit-coverage` flagged its absence on the first ship attempt: it extracted #86's stale block and correctly reported this PR's behavior as undeclared.

**Mode:** feature (SAFETY — inter-process handoff transport + a new trust boundary).

**Spec-walk:**
- [x] A forked skill and its parent shell resolve the SAME handoff path, so a handoff written by ship Step 2a is readable by `Skill("flow:audit-skips")`. Repo-local `<repo-root>/.flow/`, never `/tmp` — a fork cannot see a `/tmp` file the parent wrote. (verify: `run_scratch_isolation_evals.py` `block-*` — extracts and **executes** the real audit-skips `!`-block against a matching handoff and asserts it audits rather than emitting the absent-handoff note.)
- [x] Two different repos never share a scratch dir, and neither can read the other's handoff. (verify: `run_scratch_isolation_evals.py` `scratch-two-repos-isolated` + `stamp-foreign-refused`, two seeded temp repos.)
- [x] A handoff must prove it belongs to this repo+branch+HEAD before it is audited; `ok` / `absent` / `invalid` / `stale` / `unverifiable` stay five distinct states, and an unstamped handoff is refused (fail closed). (verify: `stamp-*` cases incl. `stamp-absent-stamp-refused`, `stamp-stale-head-refused`, `stamp-absent-distinct`, `stamp-invalid-distinct`.)
- [x] A symlinked spelling of the same repo root is NOT a mismatch — a false refusal would draft a clean PR, and a gate that cries wolf gets waived by habit. (verify: `stamp-symlink-not-a-mismatch`.)
- [x] ship Step 2a writes VALID JSON: the heredoc terminator is at column 0 and the write is read-back-asserted with `jq .` at the write site. (verify: the harness extracts and **executes** the Step 2a fence in a seeded repo and `json.loads` the product — the writer half was previously only string-matched, which is how the indented-terminator bug shipped.)
- [x] No `!`-span in any shipped `SKILL.md` contains an inner backtick (one truncates the span, so everything after it is emitted as literal text instead of executing). (verify: `span-intact-*`, blanket over every `SKILL.md`, one assertion per span.)
- [x] No cross-boundary `/tmp/flow-*` path survives anywhere a consumer copies — including `template/` and `*.json`, not just `skills/`+`agents/`. The guard tests LIVE USE (assignment / redirect / JSON value), so documenting the fix stays legal. (verify: `contract-no-tmp-survivors`; falsifiability confirmed by reintroducing the template survivor and watching it fail.)
- [x] `.flow/` self-ignores from the shell sites that actually run — not only from the Python helper, which no shipped path calls. (verify: `scratch-self-ignores` asserted against the EXECUTED shell idiom + `contract-*-self-ignores` at all five sites.)
- [x] A `referenceGlob` resolving zero documents warns loudly instead of rendering no `## Reference documents` section, and flow's own config sets the slot. (verify: `extract_session.py --reference-glob` with a matching and a non-matching glob; the warning names the configuration failure and tells the reviewer it cannot raise a Spec violation.)
- [x] **Reviewer workspace-identity path** (a second, independent mechanism from the JSON stamp): each reviewer diff carries a leading `# flow-review-context repo=… branch=… head=…` line, `/flow:staff-review` passes that identity to every lens as an explicit input, and a lens whose header disagrees **stops and reports** rather than reviewing or silently regenerating. A matching run must NOT halt. (verify: contract-greps that all three reviewer SKILLs emit the header and that staff-review's input list names the identity; all four `lens-*.md` instruct the compare-and-stop. Dogfooded live this ship — all four lenses confirmed the header before reviewing. The prepended non-diff line is safe for the consumers that exist: the lenses `Read` the patch, nothing `git apply`s it.)
- [x] **No-worktree (detached) behavior is declared per site, and differs deliberately:** `ship` Step 2a **exits 1** (a handoff a fork cannot read is no gate, so it must fail loud, not degrade); the four reviewer sites fall back to `${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/flow-detached` (a detached run has no repo identity, so it has no cross-run continuity to protect); `rigor-marker.py` falls back to a tempdir path so a detached run still functions. The shared `flow-detached` directory is the one residual global path and is **named in `roadmap.md` § Exploration** rather than left implicit. (verify: `scratch-detached-distinct` executes the shell idiom from a non-repo cwd and asserts the fallback is reached and is not a repo-shaped `.flow`; the ship `exit 1` is contract-grepped.)
- [x] **Scratch is never written through a symlink** (CWE-59): **every** `.flow/` writer refuses when the scratch dir is a symlink — the five shell sites plus the two Python writers (`rigor-marker.py` `_default_path`, `manifest-triage.py` `_repo_scratch`). `/flow:audit-coverage` caught that the criterion said "five" while `rigor-marker.py` was an undeclared sixth writer, so a tester verifying it would have passed while the class stayed open. Moving the sink from `/tmp` into repo-controlled namespace introduced this — an untrusted clone can ship `.flow` as a symlink, and `mkdir -p` on an existing symlink-to-dir exits 0 and follows it. (verify: contract-grep that each of the five sites carries the `[ -L "$FLOW_SCRATCH" ]` refusal before its `mkdir -p`; the primitive was reproduced by hand before fixing.)
- [x] **A malformed stamp refuses instead of raising**, and the handoff stamp is built with `jq -n --arg` so a legal-but-hostile branch name (a `"` — `git check-ref-format` permits it) cannot inject JSON structure. (verify: `check_stamp` with non-string `repo` returns `(False, reason)` for int/list/dict/bool; the Step 2a fence executed in a repo whose branch is `feat"x` produces valid JSON with the quote preserved.)
- [x] `#85`'s `manifest-triage.py` writes its manifest + state repo-locally, not to `/tmp`. The manifest is not a cache — producers append and `classify` reads it to decide draft-vs-ready — so one foreign entry on a same-named branch would permanently draft an unrelated clean PR. (verify: `contract-no-tmp-survivors`, whose scan now globs `*.py`/`*.sh` and `scripts/` — it was structurally blind to Python and that is exactly how this survived the merge.)
- [x] Every `[skip-audit]` manifest producer line parses under `manifest-triage.py`'s `_LINE_RE` (a literal ` — needs: ` separator) and carries a `confidence:` axis plus an explicit `human-waive` option, per `#85`. Two templates silently dropped before this — `parse_entries` does `if not m: continue`, so a followed instruction produced an entry `classify` never saw → empty manifest → a **ready** PR on a gate that never ran. (verify: `run_manifest_triage_evals.py` producer-shape cases, green.)
- [x] `run_scratch_isolation_evals.py` is wired into `ci.yml` and self-asserts that wiring; all 21 harnesses green. (verify: `ci-wired` + the full suite.)

**Explicitly OUT:** the artifact-less-reviewer breadcrumb, detached-HEAD stamp passage, the `flow-detached` global fallback, and the zero-reference-docs opt-out sentinel — all four routed to `roadmap.md` § Exploration with named shapes. Part A (verdict provenance + `UNDETERMINED`) was substantially pre-empted by `#86` and needs re-scoping against it.



## Retained detail — shipped PRs (below the active block above)

**▶ Prior (v1.25.0, shipped #90): `/flow:land` becomes model-invocable so `/flow:post-merge` can actually call it (FB-0077).** `/flow:post-merge` §3 — the composition step that makes it an orchestrator rather than a reminder — never ran. `/flow:land` carried `disable-model-invocation: true`, so the `Skill("flow:land")` call was rejected on every invocation since v1.21.0. FB-0074 (v1.22.0) caught the broken call and added `doctor/lib/skill-composition-lint.py` (a correct static detector for a contract split across two files, the FB-0010 fan-out class) — but then satisfied its own lint by **deleting the call** and rewriting §3 as a hand-off to the human, conceding the feature instead of fixing it. Surfaced live this session when `/flow:post-merge 84` reported land as an outstanding manual step and the user asked why. Fix: clear the flag (it was redundant — land's §1a gate already refuses any PR `gh` doesn't report as merged, and Claude cannot merge, so the auto-fire case was unreachable), state the never-auto-fire intent as a new **§0 precondition** naming the two legitimate entries, restore the real call in §3, and keep `/flow:post-merge`'s own flag as the human gate above the path. The lint's remediation text now leads with the redundancy question rather than "hand it to the human," which is the advice that produced the wrong fix. Pinned by **5 new live assertions** in `run_skill_composition_evals.py` (layer 3) — the lint alone passes both when the composition is legal *and* when the call was deleted, which is precisely how the first fix slipped through; all 5 mutation-tested (5/5 caught, files restored byte-identical). See history.md + FB-0077.

**Spec-walk (retained — shipped v1.25.0 as #90; heading qualified so the walk parsers stop at the ACTIVE block above, per `plan-discipline.md` § Active-block placement):** (declared retroactively at ship — the work was directed conversationally rather than through the plan gate, and `/flow:audit-skips` correctly refused the "no Spec-walk" skip. Each box records how it was actually verified; `platform: library` means there is no runnable target, so verification is mechanical rather than behavioral.)

**▶ This branch (version-neutral, on v1.25.0): the `/plugin` pane description is a UI surface, not an append-only changelog — and not a skill catalog either (FB-0082).** The `description` fields in `plugin.json` + `marketplace.json` — the text Claude Code renders in the Plugins pane — had reached **27,711 / 25,795 / 17,461 characters** because every version bump since v1.2.3 appended its release blurb there instead of to `CHANGELOG.md` (33 versions named in the plugin field alone, v1.2.3 → v1.25.0). Now **216 / 216 / 85**. The first cut kept a list of all 17 `/flow:*` skills; the user challenged that premise and the docs settled it — **Claude Code generates the component inventory from disk** for Discover's "Will install" section and the Installed detail view, so a hand-written copy is redundant *and* staleable. Calibrated against Anthropic's own official marketplace (276 plugins: median 176 chars, p90 312, 1 with a version token). The durable half is the guard — new CI-wired `evals/run_plugin_desc_evals.py` (19 checks, each mutation-tested): caps calibrated on that corpus, **a ban on `vN.N.N` tokens** (the append habit always opened with one, so regrowth fails at sentence 1 rather than at 27KB), **a ban on enumerating skills**, plugin.json↔marketplace parity, version parity — plus the rule written into `.claude/rules/safety.md` and the dev-side `/ship`. `run_land_evals` + `run_merge_status_evals` had assertions *enforcing* the catalog; both retargeted to the real catalog sites (`docs/workflow.md`, `workflow-help`). No version bump: a presentation fix to an existing release. Swept the class it names — the same version-token ban now also runs over all 17 skills' and 9 agents' frontmatter `description:` (green today; it caught one false positive first — `/flow:doctor` legitimately cites "the v1.2+ schema" — so frontmatter requires a complete three-component release while display copy does not). **Found in passing, not fixed:** `README.md` omits `/flow:contribute` + `/flow:land`, `workflow-help` omits `/flow:audit-skips` + `/flow:contribute` — a generalized catalog-coverage eval over the three catalog sites is the right fix and is its own PR. Renumbered FB-0077 → FB-0082 at the ship-time rebase (#90 claimed 0077). See history.md.

**▶ This branch (v1.26.0): one slot can't answer two questions — split `uiFilePatterns` into `visualFilePatterns` + `a11yFilePatterns` (FB-0079).** `uiFilePatterns` gates two reviewers that ask **different** questions of the same diff: `visual-significance.py` asks *"does this change what the app draws?"*, `/flow:accessibility-review` asks *"does this change something with an accessibility surface?"*. Those sets are not the same set, so every scoping choice a consumer makes is a forced trade in one direction. Measured on a real iOS/SwiftUI consumer (health-tracker PR #100): `Insight/` had to be **included** for a11y, because it builds the string VoiceOver reads — which forces `Insight/InsightCacheStore.swift`, pure persistence with no render path, to over-flag *visually*. `Data/MockSleep.swift` had to be **excluded** because it has no a11y surface — but it decides what the hypnogram looks like, so the visual half needed a hand-authored `**Visual-walk:**` block as a workaround. With one slot, neither question is expressible; the consumer picks which reviewer to lie to. This is also the root cause behind 2 of the ~6 draft-manifest items measured in FB-0075 (one `uiFilePatterns` misconfiguration surfacing as two unrelated-looking blockers).

**▶ Next up: the remaining items from the health-tracker PR #100 consumer report.** #95 closed the first and highest-value of four upstream fixes that cold-run surfaced; **item 1 below merged as #116 (v1.29.0)**. The other two are independent of each other and were re-verified against HEAD before #95 started, so each is ready to plan:

1. **Binary asset replacement computes `visual_significant: false`. — ✅ MERGED as #116 (v1.29.0, FB-0086).** `_diff_content_changed` tracks the current file via the `+++ b/<path>` header, and git emits none for a modified binary — only `Binary files a/f.ttf and b/f.ttf differ`. Reproduced against v1.25.0: an in-place font re-export (`M …/Fraunces.ttf`) reads `false` while adding a new font reads `true`. That matters because a real font PR *is* a re-export at the same path. Note the function now returns `(changed, debug_only_skipped)` and takes `visual_re` — write the fix against that signature. Parsing both sides of the `Binary files …` line also covers deletions, which have the same blind spot (`+++ /dev/null`).
2. **`skills/ship/lib/verify-pr-body.sh` — the resolver and the prose guard.** The queued contribution claiming `flow_verify_pr_write` "fails OPEN (returns 0)" is **wrong and was wrong when filed** — verified against `git show 46f8b93`: the resolver has returned `1` and every caller `2` since the file was introduced at v1.17.0. Mark that entry incorrect rather than superseded. The two real issues: the resolver only checks `$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` and the in-repo relative path, so a correctly-installed plugin sourced from a shell with neither can't find its own sibling (reproduced: `RC=2`, fails closed but unusable) — resolve relative to the sourced script's own location, gating the bash array syntax behind `eval` so `sh`/`zsh` don't choke on parsing; and the `|| exit 1` guard is prose in **two** places (`ship/SKILL.md` ~857 and ~1205), so correctness depends on the agent typing it at each call site. Add halting wrappers plus a lint over the call sites; keep the return-code forms for `/flow:doctor` and `/flow:land`, which want a code, not a halt.
3. **Shared `/tmp` artifact paths collide across concurrent worktrees.** `verifyFindingsPath`, `verifyReportPath`, `flow-staff-*`, `flow-{sec,a11y}-*`, `flow-skip-audit-stages`, `flow-pr-body`, `flow-triage`. This happened live at PR #100 — another branch overwrote the buffer between write and render, and only `/flow:audit-skips`'s branch+sha freshness check caught it. Scope the defaults through one shared resolver; the precedent already exists at `manifest-triage.py`'s `_slug(branch)`. Four queued contribution entries cover this ground — dedupe into one fix rather than layering. **It reproduced again during `/flow:land 95`, in a new place:** land's §5 visual-history gate tests only `[ -f "$FINDINGS" ]` and opened on a buffer whose `metadata.branch` was `claude/sleep-metrics-stage-colour-9624b9` — a *different project's* branch, sitting in the shared `/tmp/flow-verify-findings.json`. Following the gate naively would have distilled another project's visual decision into flow's own visual-history. `/flow:audit-skips` has the branch+sha freshness check that caught the PR #100 instance; land's §5 does not. So the fix has two halves, and the second is the one that generalizes: scope the default paths, **and** give every consumer that reads a shared artifact the same freshness assertion — a path fix alone still leaves the next un-scoped reader exposed.

Decided with the user: ship these as separate stacked PRs in the order above, with scoped defaults (explicit config still wins) for item 3 and halting wrappers for item 2.

**▶ Recently completed — MERGED as #116 (`fc0bdf78`, v1.29.0, FB-0086): item 1 — a modified/deleted binary asset computed `visual_significant: false`.** Re-verified against HEAD (v1.28.0, 2026-08-15): the bug reproduced exactly as the consumer reported — `M .../Fraunces.ttf` read `False`, `A .../NewFont.ttf` read `True`. Fixed, shipped as #116, and merged into `main`. Items 2 and 3 below remain.

- **Root cause.** `_diff_content_changed(diff_text, visual_re, asset_re)` (returns the `(changed, debug_only_skipped)` tuple since v1.26.0) decides which file a hunk belongs to by watching for the `+++ b/<path>` header. Git emits **no `+++` header for a modified binary** — only `Binary files a/<path> and b/<path> differ`. So `cur_relevant` never flips true, no render delta is seen, and the pure-refactor exclusion fires. A real font/icon PR *is* an in-place re-export at the same path, so it is *always* the broken case (health-tracker PR #100).
- **Fix.** Add a branch for the `Binary files … differ` line: extract **both** the `a/` and `b/` sides, strip the prefix, and if *either* side (ignoring `/dev/null`) matches `visual_re` or `asset_re`, count it as a render delta (`return True, debug_only_skipped`). Parsing both sides covers binary **add / modify / delete uniformly** — an add is `Binary files /dev/null and b/<path>`, a delete is `Binary files a/<path> and /dev/null`. The branch is self-contained (checks the parsed paths directly, not `cur_relevant`) so it does not perturb the `#if DEBUG` preprocessor tracking, which only governs text-hunk content.
- **Delete decision (argued deliberately, per the constraint note).** A binary *delete* of a matched asset counts as a render delta ⇒ significant. Rationale: removing a rendered asset (font/icon/image) changes what the app draws (a fallback renders, or the element disappears); the visual gate's fail-safe direction is to **over-demand** a screenshot, not under-demand (a false positive costs one "nothing to show" note, a false negative ships an unverified visual regression — the exact class this fix closes); and the diff cannot distinguish dead-asset cleanup from live-asset removal, so we take the safe union (consistent with FB-0079's fail-safe-direction corollary). Alternative — count only add/modify, ignore the `a/` side — rejected because it reintroduces a blind spot identical to the one this fix removes.
- **Explicit non-goal (scope discipline).** A *text*-file full deletion has a **separate** blind spot (`+++ /dev/null` + skipped `---`), not reached by the `Binary files` parser. Out of scope for item 1 (binary assets); recorded as a known limitation in history for a follow-up. Not silently pinned green.

**Spec-walk:**

- [x] An in-place binary **modify** of a matched asset (`M .../Fraunces.ttf`, `Binary files a/… and b/… differ`) reads `visual_significant: true`. *Verified:* `binary-modify-significant` — **RED pre-fix** (reads false, the reported bug), green post-fix.
- [x] A binary **delete** (`D .../Fraunces.ttf`, `Binary files a/… and /dev/null differ`) reads true. *Verified:* `binary-delete-significant` — **RED pre-fix** (D is excluded from `new_files`; the diff is blind today). Delete-counts-as-significant decision recorded in history.
- [x] **Both sides of the `Binary files … differ` line are parsed.** *Verified:* `binary-both-sides-checked` — a binary rename+modify with a NON-matching `a/` path and a MATCHING `b/` path, status M (so the `new_files` shortcut does not cover it). **RED pre-fix** *and* mutation-tested: reducing the loop to `mb.group(1)` (a-side only) makes it FAIL. This is the b-side's real red-verify — the `binary-add` case below cannot be, because an A-status file already hits the `new_files` shortcut.
- [x] A binary **add** stays significant. *Verified:* `binary-add-significant` — kept as a **regression control** (green pre- and post-fix via the pre-existing `new_files` A-status shortcut), pinning the report's `A NewFont.ttf = True` baseline. Explicitly NOT a red-verify (honesty per FB-0079 corollary 2 — a green-both-ways fixture proves nothing about the new code, so it is labelled a control, not a proof).
- [x] A binary change to a **non-matched** path reads false — the parser matches the path, it does not fire blindly on any `Binary files` line. *Verified:* `binary-nonasset-not-significant` (green control; a `.pack` outside the asset pattern).
- [x] **Regression controls unchanged (text axis):** `significant` (text modify), `asset-only`, `docs-only`, `backend-only`, `pure-refactor`, `rename-only`, `debug-only-not-significant`, `debug-else-branch-significant`, `debug-mixed-still-significant`, and all FB-0079 split-slot cases still pass. The `#if DEBUG` preprocessor tracking is untouched (the binary branch is self-contained — reads/writes neither `cur_relevant` nor the pp stack).
- [x] **Red-verify done** (the repo's bar): restored the pre-fix `visual-significance.py`, confirmed the three RED checks FAIL; restored the fix, confirmed 57/57 PASS. A guard that has never failed is not known to work.
- [x] **All CI harnesses green**, enumerated from `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (not globbed): all 24 `run_*_evals.py` + `dev-docs/check-index.py`. `run_visual_significance_evals.py` was already wired (ci.yml:62); the wired-vs-on-disk integrity check (ci.yml:34-45) stays satisfied — no new harness file, nothing to wire (re-verified MATCH).
- [x] **Explicit non-goal recorded:** a *text*-file full deletion has a separate `+++ /dev/null` blind spot the `Binary files` parser does not reach — out of scope for item 1, documented as a known limitation in history for a follow-up (not silently pinned green).
- [x] FB-0086 cleared at **merge** (#116, `fc0bdf78`); version bumped 1.28.0 → 1.29.0. Shipped via `/flow:ship`, merged into `main`, reservation cleared by `/flow:land`.

**▶ Prior (v1.26.0, shipped #95): one slot can't answer two questions — split `uiFilePatterns` into `visualFilePatterns` + `a11yFilePatterns` (FB-0079).** `uiFilePatterns` gates two reviewers that ask **different** questions of the same diff: `visual-significance.py` asks *"does this change what the app draws?"*, `/flow:accessibility-review` asks *"does this change something with an accessibility surface?"*. Those sets are not the same set, so every scoping choice a consumer makes is a forced trade in one direction. Measured on a real iOS/SwiftUI consumer (health-tracker PR #100): `Insight/` had to be **included** for a11y, because it builds the string VoiceOver reads — which forces `Insight/InsightCacheStore.swift`, pure persistence with no render path, to over-flag *visually*. `Data/MockSleep.swift` had to be **excluded** because it has no a11y surface — but it decides what the hypnogram looks like, so the visual half needed a hand-authored `**Visual-walk:**` block as a workaround. With one slot, neither question is expressible; the consumer picks which reviewer to lie to. This is also the root cause behind 2 of the ~6 draft-manifest items measured in FB-0075 (one `uiFilePatterns` misconfiguration surfacing as two unrelated-looking blockers).

Fix: two **optional** slots, each resolving `explicit slot → uiFilePatterns → DEFAULT_UI_PATTERN`, so a project that sets only `uiFilePatterns` is byte-identical to today and nothing changes without opt-in. One shared resolver (`skills/verify-build/lib/file_patterns.py`, importable underscore name alongside `walk_extract.py`) owns the chain for both Python consumers, retiring the `DEFAULT_UI_PATTERN` constant that `skip-audit-checks.py` currently keeps as a hand-synced copy (FB-0010). The subtle half is inside `/flow:audit-skips`: `touches_ui` today backs **both** the a11y-skip check (`skip-audit-checks.py:294`) and the verify-build doc-only check (`:256`), while the visual verdict arrives separately through `compute_visual_significance`. Splitting the slot without splitting that field would make the audit validate an a11y skip against the *visual* pattern — a confidently-wrong verdict measured with the wrong ruler. So `touches_ui` becomes `touches_visual` + `touches_a11y`, the a11y branch cross-checks against the a11y pattern only, and the doc-only branch takes the **union** (claiming "doc-only" must be refused if *either* surface is touched). See history.md + FB-0079.

**Spec-walk:**

- [x] A project that sets only `uiFilePatterns` gets a byte-identical verdict from `visual-significance.py` and `skip-audit-checks.py` to the pre-split code. *Verified:* `fb78-backcompat-ui-only-matches` / `-excludes` (visual) + `fb78-backcompat-shared-slot-refuses` / `-confirms` (skip-audit). All four pass against BOTH the pre-split and post-split engines — which is what back-compat means, and also why they carry no red-verify (the behavior-change cases below do).
- [x] A project that sets only `uiFilePatterns` gets a byte-identical early-exit decision from `/flow:accessibility-review`'s `grep -E` gate. *Verified:* the real gate block extracted from `SKILL.md` and run against a temp git repo, pre-split vs post-split, over `Views/Home.swift` (RAN/RAN), `Data/Store.swift` (SKIPPED/SKIPPED), `docs/guide.md` (SKIPPED/SKIPPED) — identical throughout; the `a11yFilePatterns` opt-in flips `Insight/Cache.swift` SKIPPED→RAN. `sh -n` + `bash -n` both parse the block.
- [x] `visualFilePatterns` overrides `uiFilePatterns` for the visual verdict and has **no** effect on the a11y gate. *Verified:* `fb78-visual-slot-overrides-shared` + `fb78-a11y-slot-does-not-leak-into-visual`. The first is RED pre-split; the second cannot be (pre-split has no a11y slot to leak), so it was mutation-tested — pointing the visual predicate at `A11Y` makes it fail.
- [x] `a11yFilePatterns` overrides `uiFilePatterns` for the a11y gate and has **no** effect on `visual_significant`. *Verified:* `fb78-a11y-skip-refused-when-a11y-pattern-matches` (RED pre-split: LEGITIMATE→SHOULD-RE-RUN) + the live-gate run above.
- [x] The consumer's real trade disappears: an a11y-surface-only file (`Insight/InsightCacheStore.swift` shape) makes an a11y skip illegitimate **without** making the change visually significant. *Verified:* `fb78-a11y-only-file-not-visual` — RED pre-split, reproducing the reported symptom exactly (`visual_significant: True` on `Insight/InsightCacheStore.swift`).
- [x] The mirror case: a render-only file (`Data/MockSleep.swift` shape) makes the change visually significant **while** `no UI in diff` stays a LEGITIMATE a11y skip. *Verified:* `fb78-visual-only-file-is-visual` — RED pre-split (`False` on `Data/MockSleep.swift`, the case that needed the hand-authored Visual-walk workaround).
- [x] `/flow:audit-skips` cross-checks each reviewer against the pattern *that reviewer used* — no merged field. *Verified:* `fb78-emits-split-diff-fields` (RED pre-split) asserts the emitted contract is exactly `{touches_source, touches_visual, touches_a11y}`. No live code reads `touches_ui`; the five survivors are eval comments describing the pre-split behavior.
- [x] A "doc-only" verify-build skip is refused when *either* surface is touched, not just the visual one. *Verified:* `fb78-doc-only-refused-when-only-a11y-surface-touched` — RED pre-split (LEGITIMATE→SHOULD-RE-RUN on an `.hbs` file with an a11y surface and no source extension).
- [x] Invalid regex in either new slot falls back loudly (named slot in the warning), never silently. *Verified:* `fb78-invalid-slot-warns-by-name` — RED pre-split. `TypeError` added alongside `re.error` so a non-string slot degrades instead of crashing.
- [x] Slot count fan-out is closed at 32 (FB-0010 grep-first-edit-second). *Verified:* **not by grepping the old value — that is what missed it.** `/flow:audit-coverage` falsified the first version of this criterion: `grep -rn '30 slots'` returned empty while `doctor/SKILL.md` still said `all 30\n  slots`, wrapped across a line in its frontmatter and therefore invisible to a line-oriented grep. Now pinned by `no-stale-slot-count-in-shipped-surfaces` in `run_merge_status_evals.py`, which matches the `\d+\s+slots?` SHAPE across newlines over every shipped `.md`/`.json`/`.sh`, skipping comment lines (historical narrative) — red-verified by restoring the wrapped survivor; the current-state claims in `docs/workflow.md` and `template/base/CLAUDE.md.template` read 32, and the `run_merge_status_evals.py` tripwire fired on the change and was retargeted to 32. Historical mentions in CHANGELOG/history/roadmap kept, scoped to their own versions. The fan-out is two sites smaller than when this PR was planned: #92 deleted the `(30 slots)` sentence from both manifest descriptions, so they are no longer fan-out sites at all.
- [x] The docs no longer describe the slot as shared. *Verified:* `grep -rn 'shared with a11y'` over shipped surfaces returns empty; the `verify-build/SKILL.md` table row is split into two rows and a new note states the two questions explicitly.
- [x] The cross-runtime chain (Python resolver ↔ the a11y gate's jq) is checked mechanically, not by comment. *Verified:* `fb79-jq-mirror-extractable` + 6 × `fb79-jq-matches-python:*` extract the LIVE jq from the SKILL and compare against `resolve()`. Mutation-tested twice: naive `//` semantics fails 2 parity checks; deleting the line fails the extractability check rather than passing vacuously. This guard exists because `/simplify` caught the docstring shipping the rejected jq form in the same commit that rejected it.
- [x] A **non-string** slot value (schema-invalid but hand-editable) resolves to the SAME slot in the a11y gate's jq as in `file_patterns.resolve()`. *Verified:* declared after `/flow:security-review` and `/flow:audit-coverage` both caught that the first fix was half a fix — a `type == "string"` guard in jq matched only the FALSY non-strings (`[]`, `{}`, `0`), while a truthy one (`["\\.tsx$"]`, `{"a":1}`, `1`, `true`) was still selected by Python's bare truthiness and skipped by jq. `resolve()` now tests `isinstance(val, str) and val`, the same question jq asks. Pinned by 16 `fb79-jq-matches-python:*` shapes; mutation-tested (reverting to bare truthiness fails 4).
- [x] A broken install (`lib/file_patterns.py` absent) fails **CLOSED** on `uiSurface:true` but still lets `uiSurface:false` win — the one change here that alters a ship-blocking verdict, and it had no eval at all. *Verified:* `fb79-broken-install-fails-closed:{ui,headless}` + `-names-remedy:*` move the module aside and assert exit 2, well-formed JSON, `visual_significant` tracking `ui_surface`, and a warning naming the reinstall.
- [x] The visual signal names the slot that supplied the pattern, so an operator reading verify-build output knows which knob to turn. *Verified:* `fb78-signal-names-source-slot`. Also fixed the default path, which read `diff touches built-in default: …` — ungrammatical on the most common config.
- [x] `bootstrap.sh` reports the real key count of the config it generated rather than a hardcoded literal. *Verified:* it read "28 slots" for a 22-key config; now `jq 'keys|length'`. Note this counts the generated config's keys (22), which is deliberately a different number from the schema's 32 slots.
- [x] All CI eval harnesses green. *Verified:* all 22 harnesses enumerated from `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and run; 22/22 pass (#92 added `run_plugin_desc_evals.py`). Set-compared `ci.yml` against `evals/run_*.py` — no unwired harness, no missing file.

- [x] The plugin description stays under 400 characters and the marketplace-level one under 200 — caps set above the official marketplace's measured p90 (312) for the plugin field, deliberately below it for the marketplace-level field (different population). *Verified:* `len:*` checks, one per description field; mutation-tested at 410 and 210 chars.
- [x] No version number appears in any description field. *Verified:* `no-version-token:*`. Mutation-tested across four shapes (`v1.26.0 adds`, `1.26.0 adds`, `v1.26 generalizes`, `v1.9.1 hardens`); replayed against `origin/main`'s real 27,711-char description, which it fails.

- [x] `plugin.json` and the matching marketplace entry carry identical description text. *Verified:* `desc-parity`, keyed on the entry whose `name` matches `plugin.json` — with `parity-entry-found` failing loudly on a name mismatch rather than silently falling back to `[0]`.
- [x] All three version fields across the two manifests agree. *Verified:* `version-parity`; mutation-tested by drifting `metadata.version`.
- [x] The harness is wired into `ci.yml` as an executable `- run:` step, not merely named in a comment. *Verified:* `ci-wired` (regex-scoped to a `- run:` line, matching `ci.yml`'s own join-check reasoning); mutation-tested by demoting the step to a comment.

- [x] Malformed input fails with a named check rather than a traceback. *Verified:* `manifests-parse` and `marketplace-has-plugins`; mutation-tested with invalid JSON and an explicit `"plugins": []`.
- [x] The same version-token ban covers all 17 skills' and 9 agents' frontmatter `description:`, with an unreadable file failing rather than passing as clean. *Verified:* `no-version-token:{skill,agent}-frontmatter` + `{skill,agent}-frontmatter-parsed`; mutation-tested by injecting a release token into each surface and by corrupting a frontmatter block. Uses a stricter three-component token here — `/flow:doctor` legitimately cites "the v1.2+ schema", which is a compatibility floor, not a changelog.
- [ ] **Not machine-verifiable, and deliberately left unchecked:** the `/plugin` pane renders the new description legibly. `/plugin` is an interactive terminal panel this session cannot drive, so this is a human check at the merge gate — not an assumed pass. It is the one item this PR's own rule ("never ship a rendered field you have not seen rendered") asks a person to close.

**▶ Prior (v1.25.0, shipped #90): `/flow:land` becomes model-invocable so `/flow:post-merge` can actually call it (FB-0077).** `/flow:post-merge` §3 — the composition step that makes it an orchestrator rather than a reminder — never ran. `/flow:land` carried `disable-model-invocation: true`, so the `Skill("flow:land")` call was rejected on every invocation since v1.21.0. FB-0074 (v1.22.0) caught the broken call and added `doctor/lib/skill-composition-lint.py` (a correct static detector for a contract split across two files, the FB-0010 fan-out class) — but then satisfied its own lint by **deleting the call** and rewriting §3 as a hand-off to the human, conceding the feature instead of fixing it. Surfaced live this session when `/flow:post-merge 84` reported land as an outstanding manual step and the user asked why. Fix: clear the flag (it was redundant — land's §1a gate already refuses any PR `gh` doesn't report as merged, and Claude cannot merge, so the auto-fire case was unreachable), state the never-auto-fire intent as a new **§0 precondition** naming the two legitimate entries, restore the real call in §3, and keep `/flow:post-merge`'s own flag as the human gate above the path. The lint's remediation text now leads with the redundancy question rather than "hand it to the human," which is the advice that produced the wrong fix. Pinned by **5 new live assertions** in `run_skill_composition_evals.py` (layer 3) — the lint alone passes both when the composition is legal *and* when the call was deleted, which is precisely how the first fix slipped through; all 5 mutation-tested (5/5 caught, files restored byte-identical). See history.md + FB-0077.


**▶ Prior (v1.24.0): annotation-layer v2 — commenting as a mode (FB-0076).** The `/flow:verify-build` overlay is redesigned around a persistent commenting MODE instead of a per-comment `Pin` toggle: click an element, write or dictate, `↵`, click the next one — no re-arming. Chrome collapses to ONE circular floating control (the minimized comment container; filled = live, carries the count) expanding to a panel with a labelled **Commenting** switch, hover-outlining + hide-pins toggles, per-row copy/delete, **Copy all**, two-tap **Delete all**. Modifier-click passes through to the page, a text-selection guard, and `Esc` are the escape hatches that keep an always-on mode safe on interactive prototypes. Toasts removed — each control states its own condition, with a visually-hidden `role="status"` region for screen readers. Designed across six rounds by annotating the prototype **with the prototype**. A staff design + UX lens pass then caught three silent-failure defects (capture-phase `Enter`/arrow `preventDefault` disabling every control's keyboard activation; white-on-accent failing WCAG in dark mode; 18 invalid `font:` shorthands that had been silently dropped since the layer was written). Element ids `annot-*` → `an-*` and the storage key changed, so the two evals that grep the partial were updated in the same commit (FB-0010 fan-out discipline). See history.md.

**▶ Prior (v1.21.0, shipped #80): `/flow:post-merge` skill v1 — the "merged — safe to archive?" close-out (FB-0072).** Part B of the #78/#79 capture: a human-invoked skill (`disable-model-invocation: true`) orchestrating the post-merge close-out — a **merge-queue-safe three-state merge gate** (`skills/post-merge/lib/merge-status.py`: MERGED proceed / CLOSED-unmerged fail-loud / OPEN poll up to `postMergeWaitSeconds` then a graceful "still queued") → **calls `Skill("flow:land")`** for doc-currency (composition) → merge-gate feedback synthesis into user-scope memory + the `/flow:contribute` queue (content-match dedup, no `feedbackPath` write — v1b) → `git branch -d` cleanup → an archive-safety verdict. Deterministic core pinned by `run_merge_status_evals.py` (31 checks) in CI; new `postMergeWaitSeconds` slot (30 slots); registered across README/workflow.md/workflow-help/plugin.json/marketplace.json. See the PR block below + history.md. **v1.20.0 shipped as #75** (annotation-layer generalization, FB-0071).

**▶ Prior (v1.21.1, shipped #83): audit-skips can't silently no-op on a broken handoff + Swift preflight `ls -d` glob fix (FB-0073).** Two consumer-cold-run bugs drained from the `/flow:contribute` queue and applied directly to a **ready** PR (per FB-0073 — high-confidence, eval-pinned fixes don't get parked in a draft): (1) `skills/audit-skips/lib/skip-audit-checks.py` now **exits non-zero** (stderr diagnostic, clean stdout) on a present-but-malformed handoff instead of `return 0` + `{"error":…,"stages":[]}` — so the skip-legitimacy gate can no longer read an engine failure as "clean, nothing to audit"; the SKILL routes a distinct `engine_error` (loud → draft) vs the absent-handoff `note` vs a valid-empty audit. (2) `template/stacks/swift/tools/preflight/check.sh` uses `ls -d *.xcodeproj` (bundle name) not bare `ls` (bundle contents → `-project project.pbxproj`). New `run_skip_audit_evals.py` cases; the removed `/flow:ship` Step 2a per-caller guard + the systemic fork-`/tmp`-transport question routed to `roadmap.md` § Exploration (per the `/simplify` altitude lens). See history.md "audit-skips can't silently no-op" + the Spec-walk there.

**▶ Shipped (v1.23.0, #85, `9efa9a4`): a draft PR is a last resort, not a deliverable — `/flow:ship` manifest triage + an answerable hand-off (FB-0075).** Cross-repo user report (music-app, ripe, flow): sessions keep ending with a draft PR handed back as if that were the deliverable, which a non-engineer can only answer with "now go fix it." Ship's draft decision is unconditional (`SKILL.md:888`, manifest non-empty ⇒ draft) and three problems compound into the symptom: **§7a is the one gate that never attempts a resolution** before drafting (capture + visual-history authoring are agent work — every other producer already self-resolves first: #81's rigor re-run, §2a's re-audit, §2's bounded verify-build retry); **the other seven kinds are genuine decisions written unanswerably** (`needs: declare + verify the criterion, or human waive`, no drafted resolution attached); and **there is no decision surface** — Step 8 prints a URL. Fixes, each at the site that owns it: §7a attempts-then-gates (one attempt, bounded, cleared only by its own re-assertion); every manifest entry carries a **drafted resolution** plus a plain-language "what this means / what I need from you / what happens then"; Step 8 leads with a **numbered decision list** (recommendation first, waive always offered, what was already tried shown) resolved through the existing Step 7c reconcile fast-path. Deterministic `kind → class` triage in `skills/ship/lib/manifest-triage.py`, which first required **normalizing all 8 producer sites onto one parseable line shape** (five emit `[decision-required]` where a kind belongs; four use off-vocabulary `needs:` verbs — an FB-0010 fan-out the audit gate surfaced). Per the critique gate, **no stop-before-PR gate is added** — the two-gate thesis (FB-0034/FB-0044) stays intact. See the PR block below. **v1.21.0 shipped as #80** (`/flow:post-merge` skill v1, FB-0072).

**▶ Shipped (v1.22.0, #86, `129f582`): three gates that could report success without doing their job (FB-0074).** A `/flow:contribute` drain of 12 queued lessons — 3 confirmed by reproducing the symptom, fixed, and applied to a **ready** PR (FB-0073); 2 dismissed *with* a repro; 1 escalated; 7 held. The three share a spine: *a contract whose two halves live in different files, with nothing mechanically checking the join, degrades silently — and the degradation is indistinguishable from success.* (1) **Fork root anchor (SAFETY).** `/flow:audit-coverage` + `/flow:audit-skips` are `context: fork` and inherit the *session* cwd; from a non-repo cwd every relative read returned empty and every unverifiable skip validated as `LEGITIMATE` — the gate printed "all skips LEGITIMATE — proceed" having read nothing, and "could not locate the repo" was byte-identical to "nothing to audit". Both now resolve an explicit root (`CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`, else git toplevel) before any relative read and emit a distinct `ROOT-UNRESOLVED`/`root_error` that ship Step 2a routes to `[decision-required]`; the residual foreign-repo case is *named* (`repo root: <path>`), not silently closed. (2) **Test-plan provenance (SAFETY).** The `## Test plan` was *specified* non-forgeable but zero consumers checked it; `render-test-plan.py` now stamps all three emitted paths and ship Step 7b asserts the stamp on the **re-fetched** body — the stamp attests provenance, not passage. (3) **Skill-composition lint.** `post-merge` §3 called `Skill("flow:land")` while `land` is `disable-model-invocation: true` (which blocks *programmatic* invocation — confirmed against the Claude Code reference), so that step never ran since v1.21.0; new `doctor/lib/skill-composition-lint.py` + doctor Check 1.4 fail on the shape, and §3 now hands the step to the human (land's flag is a deliberate safety property and stays). Also: `/flow:contribute` no longer drafts (FB-0073). **Escalated, not fixed:** cross-project `/tmp` handoff collisions (~16 paths) — dismissed twice before as already-encoded; the third report proved the prior reasoning was a category error (the cited guard covers 1 path, and `repo_root`, not branch+sha, is the right discriminator). Full evidence table in `roadmap.md` § Exploration. 2 new eval harnesses wired into CI; every new fixture verified to FAIL on the pre-fix tree. See history.md "three gates that could report success" + FB-0074.

**▶ Prior (v1.21.0, shipped #80): `/flow:post-merge` skill v1 — the "merged — safe to archive?" close-out (FB-0072).** Part B of the #78/#79 capture: a human-invoked skill (`disable-model-invocation: true`) orchestrating the post-merge close-out — a **merge-queue-safe three-state merge gate** (`skills/post-merge/lib/merge-status.py`: MERGED proceed / CLOSED-unmerged fail-loud / OPEN poll up to `postMergeWaitSeconds` then a graceful "still queued") → calls `/flow:land` for doc-currency (**corrected twice**: v1.21.0 specified `Skill("flow:land")` but it was rejected at runtime and never executed; v1.22.0 replaced it with a hand-off to the human; **v1.25.0/FB-0077 restored the call** by clearing land's flag, which is what the fix should have been the first time) → merge-gate feedback synthesis into user-scope memory + the `/flow:contribute` queue (content-match dedup, no `feedbackPath` write — v1b) → `git branch -d` cleanup → an archive-safety verdict. Deterministic core pinned by `run_merge_status_evals.py` (31 checks) in CI; new `postMergeWaitSeconds` slot (30 slots); registered across README/workflow.md/workflow-help/plugin.json/marketplace.json. See the PR block below + history.md. **v1.20.0 shipped as #75** (annotation-layer generalization, FB-0071).

**▶ Prior (v1.20.0, shipped #75): the verify-build walkthrough's annotation layer anchors to ANY DOM element, not just a captured screenshot (FB-0071).** The v1.7.0 click-to-pin overlay (`lib/annotation-layer.html`, FB-0051) keyed each pin to `{frame, x%, y%}` inside one `<img>` and bound its click handler only to screenshot wrappers — a paragraph, a table cell, a verdict card, or an open-question item could never carry a note. Upstreamed health-tracker's DOM-general version (its FB-0011): a DevTools-style hover-inspect picker (`elementFromPoint` + a `pickTarget()` walk-up skipping non-visual tags / the layer's own chrome / sub-8px boxes → an outline snapped to `getBoundingClientRect()`), keyboard **↑/↓** parent/child traversal (descent-stack, so Down reverses the most-recent Up), a **stable content-derived anchor** (`data-pin-id` else nearest-heading + tag + role + text-sample — never DOM index, so pins survive the report's per-iteration regeneration), a **location-descriptor export** (`## section` → `at <element> "text"`) replacing raw coordinates, and overlay-marker pins (no per-image host wrapper). `render-report.py` now injects the layer on **every** report (dropped the `annot-shot` gate — text-only reports are annotatable too). Shipped as a **reusable, self-contained partial** any flow skill/subagent emitting reviewable HTML can inject. Kept the `file://` hardening and, per FB-0071/health-tracker's reference, adopted the embedded-browser constraints the FB-0051 layer diverged from: **no native `confirm/alert/prompt`** (two-step inline Clear), **`execCommand` clipboard** (dropped async Clipboard API), localStorage, numbered pins, inline editor, Copy-notes; legacy image-pins migrate on load. Contract-grep evals in `run_report_render_evals.py` (layer-contract + always-injected) + a full in-app-browser interaction pass (picker/traversal/commit/anchor/export/migration driven via dispatched events with injected geometry — the preview's JS viewport reports zero width). SAFETY (modal/clipboard/persistence). See `history.md` "Generalize the walkthrough annotation layer" + FB-0071.

**▶ Prior (v1.19.0): lesson-harvest Step 4c added to `/flow:ship-spike` (FB-0059 parity).** `/flow:ship-spike`'s Step 4 ran only the memory self-feedback sub-steps — there was **no** Step 4c anywhere through v1.18.0 — so spikes never routed lessons about *flow itself* (gate misfires, reviewer false-positives, overruled taste calls) to the cross-project contribution queue, even though spikes are the *highest-yield* source for them. Added Step 4c to `/flow:ship-spike`, a faithful mirror of `/flow:ship` § 4c: the same `harvest_lesson.py` prescan→enqueue→mark path, the same three harvest slots, always-run; no script or schema-shape change. Staff-review (4 lenses, no BLOCKER) + `/simplify` caught a fan-out — the schema slot-descriptions were updated to "+ /flow:ship-spike" but the identical claim in `contribute`/`doctor`/`CLAUDE.md` prose was missed (fixed) — and routed the deeper 4c-body de-duplication to `roadmap.md` § Next. Security review clean; a11y/verify-build self-skip (`uiSurface:false` / `platform:library`); skip-audit all-legitimate. **Renumbered v1.17.0 → v1.18.0 → v1.19.0** across three rebases as `#71`/`#70`/`#73` each claimed the next version concurrently (caught by the stale-base gate + CI-conflict monitor each time). See `history.md` "Add lesson-harvest Step 4c". **v1.18.0 shipped as #73** (plan-file review + Spec-walk pinning lint, FB-0068).

**▶ Shipped (v1.18.0, #73): plan reviewers can review a plan DOCUMENT on disk (`--plan-file`) + a deterministic Spec-walk pinning lint (FB-0068, SAFETY).** `/flow:critique-plan <path>` / `/flow:audit-plan <path>` now critique/audit a queued plan *file* instead of only "the most recent plan" in the session transcript: `extract_session.py --plan-file` (plan-mode only; cwd-scoped path guard mirroring reference docs; fatal on missing/empty; session context best-effort with a loud standalone-review note and artifact status `UNKNOWN` not `UNREAD` so an absent transcript can't mint false unverified-recall findings). And `skills/critique-plan/lib/walk-pin-lint.py` (reusing the shared `walk_extract` parser) reports Spec-walk checkboxes naming no test/verification artifact — advisory input to the critic, severity gated on a documented pinning rule. Both surfaced dogfooding queued plan documents in a **consumer workspace where the plugin was not installed** (reviewer prompts applied by reference, not dispatched); the `--plan-file` gap is invocation-independent. `run_plan_file_evals.py` (21) + `run_pin_lint_evals.py` (24) wired into CI. See history.md "Plan reviewers can review a plan document" + FB-0068.

**▶ Prior (v1.17.0, shipped #70): PR body↔draft coherence + read-back-verify — a ready PR can no longer keep the `🚫 NOT READY TO MERGE` manifest (FB-0067, SAFETY).** A dogfooded PR came out `isDraft:false, MERGEABLE, CLEAN` but its body still opened with the draft manifest — the PR contradicting its own state, a recurring class. Three converging fixes, all in flow: (1) a **mandatory read-back after EVERY PR-body/draft-state write** — new `skills/ship/lib/verify-pr-body.sh` (→ deterministic `skills/ship/lib/pr-coherence.py`) re-fetches the live PR and asserts the write took (intended substrings present, manifest absent on a ready PR, draft state as intended); a `gh` write is its own checked statement, never piped into an exit-code-masking filter; (2) a **body↔draft coherence invariant** (`NOT isDraft ⇒ no manifest`) enforced at `/flow:ship` Step 7b (final pre-handoff gate), surfaced by `/flow:doctor` Check 2.10 (any open PR for HEAD), and blocked by `/flow:land` (a PR that merged carrying the manifest); (3) a **reconcile-only ship fast-path** (Step 7c) for clearing a blocker out-of-band without hand-editing the body. Pinned by `run_pr_coherence_evals.py` (wired into CI). **Renumbered twice** during this ship: drafted as v1.15.0/FB-0065, bumped to v1.16.0/FB-0066 after `#68` claimed those first, then bumped again to v1.17.0/FB-0067 after a second concurrent branch (`#71`, frame-integrity gate) *also* independently claimed v1.16.0/FB-0066 and merged first — both collisions caught by the stale-base + reserved-numbers gates on rebase, not silently shipped. **v1.16.0 shipped as #71** (frame-integrity gate — a must-pass visual checklist audits every captured screenshot independent of declared criteria, FB-0066). **v1.15.0 shipped as #68** (`/flow:ship` + `/flow:ship-spike` PR description opens with a plain-language summary + Scope label, FB-0065). **v1.14.0 shipped as #64** (orientation-doc-staleness gap — `/flow:ship` Step 5a.5 discovers UNDECLARED status surfaces that drifted after a merge → draft manifest; `statusSurfaceCandidates` slot + `lib/status-surface-scan.py` + doctor Check 2.9, FB-0064).

PR #55). v1.9.0 (#54) added statusDocs — project-declared status-surface reconciliation + a version-manifest-independent doc-currency gate. v1.8.0 shipped V3b — the durable `visual-history.html` record + the `/flow:ship` Step 5c distill bridge; v1.8.1 (#53) fixed its §5c asset-path doubling, validated by the FB-0016 iOS cold-run.** The managed-autonomy spine is shipped (two-gate loop, `/flow:verify-build` behavioral gate, autonomous ship-readiness trigger, draft-routing, `## Flow run` PR table). The **"enforce that the work was done correctly" pair is complete on `main` (v1.6.0)**: **PR TP** (#46, v1.5.3) renders the PR Test plan non-forgeably from the verify-build buffer; **PR-2** (#47, v1.6.0) adds `/flow:audit-coverage` (under-declaration → draft). **Deliverable-quality track V2 (rendered capture) + V3a (ephemeral HTML walkthrough)** shipped in **PR #45 (v1.6.1)** — a11y-gated capture-and-persist per `Visual-walk` state (iOS via XcodeBuildMCP), the stdlib HTML renderer, additive `grounding` + `open_questions` schema fields, `verifyReportPath` slot. **v1.7.0** layered the **two-way annotation delta** onto #45's renderer (click-to-pin overlay; located pins + "Copy notes"); **v1.7.1** was a plain-language copy pass on the report (#50, FB-0052); and **v1.8.0** shipped **V3b** — the durable `visual-history.html` record + the `/flow:ship` Step 5c distill bridge (a `visualHistoryPath` slot, the stdlib `insert-visual-history.py` + `visual-history-skeleton.html`, the gated distill step, the Step 4a open-question→FB extension; created-on-first-write, FB-0053 reversing FB-0042(e)'s mechanism); **v1.8.1** (#53) fixed its §5c asset-path doubling (validated by the first FB-0016 iOS cold-run). **The V1→V3 chain is shipped + cold-run-validated; only V4 remains** (`roadmap.md` § Now). **PR SD (v1.9.0, this session)** adds the `statusDocs` doc-currency mechanism — orthogonal to the Deliverable-quality track.

**Previously at v1.25.0 (`/flow:land` model-invocable so `/flow:post-merge` §3 can actually call it — the composition step had silently never run since v1.21.0; FB-0077, shipped #90. This branch is version-neutral: the `/plugin` pane `description` trimmed 27,711 → 216 chars + guarded by `run_plugin_desc_evals.py`, FB-0082). Previously v1.24.0 (annotation-layer v2 — commenting as a mode, FB-0076; before that v1.21.0 `/flow:post-merge` skill v1 — the human-invoked "merged — safe to archive?" close-out: a merge-queue-safe three-state merge gate + a call to `/flow:land` for doc-currency + merge-gate feedback synthesis into user-scope stores + branch cleanup + an archive-safety verdict; `postMergeWaitSeconds` slot, 30 slots; FB-0072; shipped #80). v1.20.0 (the `/flow:verify-build` walkthrough annotation layer generalizes from screenshot-only to ANY DOM element — a DevTools-style hover-inspect picker + ↑/↓ keyboard traversal + a regeneration-stable content-derived anchor + a location-descriptor export, injected on EVERY rendered report as a self-contained reusable partial; FB-0071; #75). v1.19.0 (lesson-harvest Step 4c added to `/flow:ship-spike` — FB-0059 parity; #72). v1.18.0 (plan-file review + Spec-walk pinning lint, FB-0068; #73). v1.17.0 (PR body↔draft coherence + read-back-verify, FB-0067; #70). v1.16.0 (frame-integrity gate — must-pass checklist on every captured screenshot, FB-0066; #71). v1.15.0 (PR description opens with a plain-language summary + Scope label, FB-0065; #68). v1.14.0 (orientation-doc-staleness gap closed — `/flow:ship` Step 5a.5 discovers UNDECLARED status surfaces that drifted after a merge → draft manifest; `statusSurfaceCandidates` slot + `lib/status-surface-scan.py` + doctor Check 2.9 + fenced/seeded CLAUDE.md scaffold, FB-0064; #64). v1.13.0 (visual-deliverable gate + `/flow:audit-skips` — two failure-open ship-pipeline gaps closed, #62, FB-0062). v1.12.0 (`/flow:land` — post-merge doc-currency skill, #60, FB-0061). v1.11.1 (`/flow:ship-spike` gh-resilience fan-out, FB-0060). v1.11.0 (lesson-harvest + `/flow:contribute` loop, FB-0059). v1.10.2 (jq boolean-slot footgun fix, PR #44 brought current — `jq '.X // true'` silently inverted an explicit `verifyEnabled: false`/`uiSurface: false` opt-out; fixed all four sites with the `if .X == false` form, FB-0058). v1.10.1 (gh Projects-classic PR-write resilience — `/flow:ship` Step 7 + `/flow:staff-review` Step 7 document a REST-`PATCH`-body + ready/draft-GraphQL-mutation fallback for the `projectCards` deprecation that breaks `gh pr edit`/`ready`/`view` on classic-projects repos; a docs-only follow-up to #57's v1.10.0, the secondary item from the same dogfood report, FB-0056). v1.10.0 (verify-build/ship integrity fix — a per-criterion `provenance` field so a HAND-AUTHORED findings buffer renders as a `[~]` self-report state, never a forgeable machine `[x]`; spike's reduced rigor gated on an *explicit* `/flow:ship-spike` with a judged no-plan fallback; `/flow:ship` Step 1.0a rigor-marker gate on source-touching diffs; #57, FB-0056). v1.9.1 (V2.1 hardening — `/flow:verify-build` visual capture decoupled from Spec-walk + the `extract-visual-states.py` parser; **Previously at v1.22.0 (three gates that could report success without doing their job, each a contract split across two files with nothing checking the join: forked audit skills (`/flow:audit-coverage`, `/flow:audit-skips`) now resolve an explicit repo root before any relative read — an unresolvable root emits a distinct `ROOT-UNRESOLVED`/`root_error` routed to the draft manifest instead of rendering byte-identically to a clean skip, closing a failure-open where a gate printed "all skips LEGITIMATE" having never read the repo; the PR `## Test plan` is now genuinely non-forgeable — `render-test-plan.py` stamps every emitted path and ship Step 7b asserts the stamp on the re-fetched body, so a hand-authored block can no longer pass as a machine verdict; and a new `doctor/lib/skill-composition-lint.py` + doctor Check 1.4 fail on any `Skill()` call naming a `disable-model-invocation: true` skill — the shape `/flow:post-merge` §3 shipped against `/flow:land` since v1.21.0, which meant that call was rejected every run and doc-currency silently degraded. `/flow:contribute` now applies verified changes to a ready PR instead of parking them in a draft (FB-0073). Cross-project `/tmp` handoff collisions are documented-not-fixed in roadmap § Exploration. FB-0074; #86).** Recently shipped: **v1.21.1 (audit-skips can't silently no-op on a broken handoff — `skip-audit-checks.py` exits non-zero on a present-but-malformed handoff so the skip-legitimacy gate can't read an engine failure as "clean, nothing to audit"; ## Handoff Notes

- **▶ NEXT AGENT / next dispatch, START HERE (2026-08-25):** the active program is the flow cloud workflow — read `research/2026-08-23-flow-cloud-workflow-plan.md` (canonical) first, then Current Focus above. The next executable units are the three parallel dispatches named there. **M** front (3): Step 1 (per-subagent model-measurement harness) is orchestrator-approved and executing now — see "PR — Model-measurement harness, Step 1" at the top of the PR-block list (heading renamed from the stale "PR — Model-measurement harness (QUEUED)" this bullet originally pointed at, FB-0089) — still measurement-first (FB-0083: Opus default, Sonnet-only challenger, no Haiku, no swap without data). **AB** (attention-budget/harness-weight audit) remains scoped in `roadmap.md` § Next, untouched and open for a fresh agent to pick up independently.

  - **M — model-measurement harness — RESOLVED (2026-08-25, FB-0089):** Step 1 (per-subagent token attribution) has been picked up; see the ACTIVE block at the top of the PR-block list and the Handoff Notes entry above. This bullet's original instruction ("drafted below... move that block to the top when you pick it up") is done; retained struck-through-in-spirit rather than deleted, per plan.md's "don't delete planned items without documenting why" rule — the "why" is that FB-0089 supersedes it.
  - **AB — attention budget & harness-weight audit.** Net-new; shape in `roadmap.md` § Next "AB" + rationale in `dev-docs/research/anthropic-canon-alignment-2026-08.md` § 3. The one net-new mechanism is a periodic harness-weight audit (does each always-loaded surface/gate still earn its token cost?); the dogfood target is compacting this very `plan.md` + `roadmap.md` § Now.
  - Context: `dev-docs/research/anthropic-canon-alignment-2026-08.md` (this session's alignment check) and its companion `ai-workflow-landscape-2026-07.md`. Read both before scoping.
- **`/flow:land 84` is still outstanding** (as of 2026-08-01). `/flow:post-merge 84` ran this session and completed every step except doc-currency — which is the defect this branch fixes. #84's forward-doc reconciliation was never done, so run `/flow:land 84` and merge its `docs: land #84` PR. Note this must be typed by the human **on the currently installed plugin (1.21.0)**; once this branch ships and the plugin is reinstalled, `/flow:post-merge` will do it itself.
- **The installed plugin is several releases behind the repo** (1.21.0 installed vs 1.25.0 here). That staleness is what produced the confusing §3 failure: installed-1.21.0's post-merge instructs `Skill("flow:land")`, which the harness rejects, while the repo's copy had already been rewritten. Worth a `/plugin install flow@flow` before the next dogfood run.

- **Ready to merge (`flow-contribution` PR #67) — `Visual-walk` nudge for visual spikes:** `/flow:ship-spike` Step 2 now nudges a *visual/interaction* spike to declare a `Visual-walk` block so verify-build §5a captures frames + renders the ephemeral walkthrough (a spike with no block gets no walkthrough), and to invoke `/flow:verify-build` rather than shortcut via `simctl` (which skips the Step-10 render). Reconciled the "N/A under spike" fan-out across all four live surfaces (`ship-spike/SKILL.md`, `workflow.md` ×2, `plan-discipline.md`, `planner.md`); left the timestamped v1.5.1 changelog/history records. Nudge, not a gate — non-visual spikes stay fast. **Merges docs-only at the current v1.19.0 (no bump)** — advisory guidance about an existing field, riding the current release like #65's how-it-works.md. Four staff-lens review, no BLOCKERs; one real follow-up routed (verify-build §5a no-block wording contradiction). PR #67 marked ready. See `history.md` "Nudge a Visual-walk block…" + FB-0069 (+ FB-0070, full-pipeline-every-PR). The harder Facet-4 plan-critic enforcement (flag a `uiSurface:true` plan omitting `Visual-walk`) remains roadmapped.
- **FOLLOW-UP surfaced by the ship-review of the above (verify-build §5a no-block wording contradiction):** `plugins/flow/skills/verify-build/SKILL.md` states two conflicting things for a plan with **no `Visual-walk` block**: the §5a *activation* rule (`:259`) says "no Visual-walk block → **skip**" (⇒ frameless), while the state-set-derivation paragraph (`:266`) and `lib/extract-visual-states.py`'s docstring (`:49-51`) say "block absent → capture the **primary/launch state only**, mark the rest `not_tested`" (⇒ one degraded frame, not frameless). Since activation requires `block_count ≥ 1`, the `:266`/docstring "(block absent)" parenthetical is unreachable and should read "(block present but 0 assertions)". Not fixed in the nudge PR (scope). Owner: whoever next touches verify-build §5a — a one-line wording fix in `SKILL.md:266` + the script docstring closes it. (Surfaced by the push-further + staff-engineer lenses.)
- **Current state is in "Current Focus" above** (v1.7.0; Deliverable-quality track, ▶ V2.1 hardening + V3b next). Shipped-PR detail lives in `history.md`. These notes are pruned at each ship by the doc-currency reconciliation step (`/flow:ship` Step 5a) once the dogfood install is ≥ v1.5.2.
- **Recently shipped (2026-06-15) — the two-way annotation layer (v1.7.0):** `render-report.py` injects `annotation-layer.html` (click-to-pin overlay) before `</body>` when ≥1 captured frame is present, completing V3a as a two-way human-feedback surface; frameless reports stay read-only; unreadable layer warns + renders read-only. **Re-scoped to the additive delta** after the stale-base gate caught **#45** pre-empting ~70% of a standalone `/flow:walkthrough` version — see `history.md` "Two-way annotation layer" + **FB-0051**. Layer-implementation gotchas worth remembering: defer image sizing to the renderer's `.obs img` CSS (don't override width — it clobbers #45's `max-width:600px` cap); pin alignment is a JS measured-host-width sync, not a CSS shrink-wrap (an `inline-block`+%-width image collapses the box to ~0px). Validate any injected-overlay CSS in a real browser.

Remaining: **V2.1 hardening** (the two cold-gate follow-ups below — `extract-visual-states.py` parser + Spec-walk-heading routing fragility) and **V3b** (durable `visual-history.html` record + the distill bridge; coordinate with **#36**'s merged blueprint/FB-0042, don't duplicate it). Read `roadmap.md` § Deliverable-quality track V2/V3/V4 for sequencing.

These shape how the autonomous loop iterates toward the deliverable — read before drafting V2.

## PR — `/flow:land` becomes model-invocable so `/flow:post-merge` can call it (v1.25.0, shipped #90, FB-0077)

Shipped record. The summary lives in Current Focus § "Prior (v1.25.0, shipped #90)" and in `history.md`; the Spec-walk below is retained HERE rather than in Current Focus so the active PR's block is unambiguously first — `walk_extract` scopes to the first block, and a heading carrying a trailing parenthetical is not a terminator, so a retained block left in Current Focus silently merges into the active one.

**Spec-walk:** (for #90 — retained as its record; declared retroactively at ship — the work was directed conversationally rather than through the plan gate, and `/flow:audit-skips` correctly refused the "no Spec-walk" skip. Each box records how it was actually verified; `platform: library` means there is no runnable target, so verification is mechanical rather than behavioral.)

- [x] `/flow:land` is model-invocable — `disable-model-invocation: false` declared explicitly in frontmatter. *Verified:* `run_land_evals.py` `skill 5` (frontmatter-anchored regex), mutation-tested — flipping to `true`, deleting the line, and destroying the frontmatter block are all caught (3/3).
